# DA THUC

# 2.1. Da thurc tren m@t vanh nguyén

Cho $K$ la mot trong cac tap só: $K = \mathbb { Z }$ $\mathbb { Q }$ ， $\mathbb { R }$ ， $\mathbb { C }$ hay $\mathbb { Z } _ { p }$ ：

V6i moi day $( a _ { 0 } , a _ { 1 } , . . . , a _ { n } , . . . )$ cac phan tur $a _ { n } \in K$ . Biéu thurc

$$
p ( x ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } x + . . . + a _ { n } x ^ { n } , a _ { n } \neq 0
$$

duoc goi la da thurc bac $n$ cua bién (hay an) $x$ . Cac s6 $a _ { 0 } , a _ { 1 } , . . . , a _ { n }$ duoc goi la cac hé só cua da thurc.

Néu $a _ { 0 } = a _ { 1 } = . . . = a _ { n } = 0$ thi ta duoc cac da thurc khong, ky hieu la 0. Tap hop cac da thurc bién $x$ voi he só thu@c $K$ duoc ky hieu la $K [ x ]$

Cho hai da thirc

$$
p ( x ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } x + . . . + a _ { n } x ^ { n } , a _ { n } \neq 0 ; q ( x ) = b _ { 0 } + b _ { 1 } x + . . . + b _ { m } x ^ { m } , b _ { m } \neq 0 .
$$

Hai da thurc $p ( x ) , q ( x )$ bang nhau duoc ky hiéu va dinh nghia nhu sau:

$$
p ( x ) = q ( x ) \Leftrightarrow \left\{ { \begin{array} { l } { m = n } \\ { a _ { 0 } = b _ { 0 } , . . . , a _ { n } = b _ { n } } \end{array} } \right. .
$$

# 2.2. Vanh da thirc

Trong tap $K [ x ]$ , gia sur $p ( { x } ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } { x } + . . . + a _ { n } { x } ^ { n } , \ a _ { n } \neq 0 ;$

$$
\begin{array} { r } { q ( x ) = b _ { 0 } + b _ { 1 } x + \ldots + b _ { m } x ^ { m } , b _ { m } \neq 0 . } \end{array}
$$

Ta dinh nghia tong $p ( x ) + q ( x )$ va tich $p ( x ) q ( x )$ cua hai da thurc $p ( x ) , q ( x )$ （20 nhu sau: $p ( x ) + q ( x ) : = ( a _ { 0 } + b _ { 0 } ) + ( a _ { 1 } + b _ { 1 } ) x + ( a _ { 2 } + b _ { 2 } ) x ^ { 2 } + . . . .$ （204号

（2 $p ( x ) q ( x ) : = c _ { 0 } + c _ { 1 } x + . . . + c _ { n + m } x ^ { n + m } , c _ { k } = \sum _ { j = 1 } ^ { k } a _ { j } b _ { k - j } , k = 0 , 1 , . . . , n + m .$ Voi quy uoc $a _ { j } = 0$ néu $j > n$ va $b _ { j } = 0$ néu $j > m$ ：

Ta có thé chirng minh duoc $\left( K [ x ] , + , \cdot \right)$ la mot vanh nguyen.

$$
\begin{array} { r l } & { \mathsf { b a c } \big ( p ( x ) + q ( x ) \big ) \le \mathsf { m a x } \big ( \mathsf { b a c } p ( x ) , \mathsf { b a c } q ( x ) \big ) . } \\ & { \quad \mathsf { b a c } \big ( p ( x ) q ( x ) \big ) \le \mathsf { b a c } p ( x ) + \mathsf { b a c } q ( x ) . } \end{array}
$$

# 2.3.Phép chia da thtrc - Nghiém

Dinh ly 1: Voi hai da thicc bat ky $p ( x ) , q ( x ) ; q ( x ) \neq 0$ déu ton tai duy nhat hai da thirc $s ( x ) , r ( x )$ sao cho $p ( x ) = q ( x ) s ( x ) + r ( x )$ ，trong do hoac $r ( x ) = 0$ hoac $r ( x ) \not = 0 \nu \dot { a } \ b \mathrm { \hat { a } c } r ( x ) < \mathrm { b \hat { a } c } q ( x )$ ，

Dinh nghia 1: $r ( x )$ dugc goi la du cua phep chia $p ( x )$ cho $q ( x )$ . Néu $r ( x ) = 0$ thi ta noi $p ( x )$ chia hét cho $q ( x )$ hay $q ( x )$ la uoc cua $p ( x )$ ：

Dinh nghia _2: $\mathit { S } \mathit { \acute { o } }$ $c \in K$ thoa man $p ( c ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } c + . . . + a _ { n } c ^ { n } = 0$ dugc goi ld nghiém cua da thirc $p ( x ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } x + . . . + a _ { n } x ^ { n }$

Dinh ly 2: Phan du cua phép chia $p ( x )$ cho $x - c$ la $p ( c )$ ：

He qua 3: $\boldsymbol { c }$ la nghiem cüa da thirc $p ( x ) k h i$ va chi khi $x - c$ la uoc cua $p ( x )$ ， nghia la $p ( x ) = \left( x - c \right) ^ { k } s ( x )$ ：

Néu $p ( x ) = \left( x - c \right) ^ { k } s ( x )$ (k nguyen, $k \geq 1$ ）va $s ( c ) \neq 0$ thi $c$ dugc goi langhiem boi $k$ cua da thic $p ( x )$ ：

Dinh nghia 3: Da thirc $p ( x ) \in K { \big [ } x { \big ] }$ dugc goi la bat kha quy néu bac $p ( x ) { \geq } 1$ vd néu $p ( x ) = q ( x ) s ( x )$ thi mot trong hai da thirc $q ( x ) , s ( x )$ la hang só khac O cua （20 $K$ , nghia la $p ( x )$ chi chia het cho $k p ( x )$ ,voi $k \in K \backslash \{ 0 \}$ ：

Chäng han: Moi da thirc bac nhat la bat kha quy. Da thirc bac $\geq 2$ bat kha quy khi va chi khi nó vo nghiém trong $K$ ：

Dinh ly 4: Moi da thirc bac $\geq 1$ trén truong só phic $\mathbb { C }$ deu co nghiem.

He qua 5: Moi da thirc $p ( x ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } x + . . . + a _ { n } x ^ { n } \in \mathbb { C } \big [ x \big ]$ déu co thé phan tich thanh $p ( x ) = a _ { n } ( x - x _ { 1 } ) . . . ( x - x _ { n } )$ , trong do cac so phic $x _ { k }$ co th $\acute { e }$ trung nhau.

He qua 6: Moi da thicc hé $s \acute { \sigma }$ thuc $p ( x ) = a _ { 0 } + a _ { 1 } x + . . . + a _ { n } x ^ { n } \in \mathbb { R } \big [ x \big ]$ co theé phan tich thanh tich cac da thirc bat kha quy la da thirc bac nhat hay da thirc bac 2 co biet thirc $\Delta$ am: $p ( x ) = a _ { n } ( x ^ { 2 } + b _ { 1 } x + c 1 ) ^ { k _ { 1 } } . . . ( x ^ { 2 } + b _ { m } x + c _ { m } ) ^ { k _ { m } } ( x - x _ { 1 } ) ^ { l _ { 1 } } . . . ( x - x _ { s } ) ^ { l _ { s } }$ voi ${ b _ { j } } ^ { 2 } - 4 c _ { j } < 0 , { j = 1 , . . . , m } ; 2 k _ { 1 } + . . . + 2 k _ { m } + l _ { 1 } + . . . + l _ { s } = n .$ （20

# 2.4. Uoc chung lon nhat, nguyen tó cung nhau

Dinh nghia 4: Néu hai da thirc $p ( x ) , \ q ( x )$ cung chia hét cho da thirc $d ( x )$ thi $d ( x )$ duoc goi la uoc chung cua $p ( x ) , q ( x )$ . Ngoai ra neu moi uoc cua $p ( x ) , q ( x )$ （204号 deu la uoc cua $d ( x )$ thi $d ( x )$ goi la uoc chung lon nhat cua $p ( x ) , q ( x ) . K \dot { y } h i \hat { e } u$ ：

$$
d ( x ) = U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) .
$$

Néu $d ( x )$ la hang só khac khong thi $p ( x ) , \ q ( x )$ dugc goi la nguyen to cing nhau. Ky hiéu $( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) = 1 .$

Chu y rang $d ( x ) = U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) )$ xac dinh duy nhat sai khac mot hang só khác O. Nghia la

$\mathsf { D } \hat { \mathsf { e } } \mathrm { t i m } \ U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) )$ ta thuc hien phép chia Euclide nhu sau:   
》 Gia sur bac $p ( x ) \geq { \mathsf { b a c } } q ( x )$ thi $p ( x ) = q ( x ) s _ { 1 } ( x ) + r _ { 1 } ( x )$ ，bac $r _ { 1 } ( x ) < \mathbf { b a c } q ( x )$   
》 Néu $r _ { 1 } ( x ) = 0 \mathrm { \ t h i } q ( x ) = U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) ,$   
》Néu $r _ { 1 } ( x ) \neq 0$ lap lap qua trinh tren doi voi $q ( x )$ va $r _ { 1 } ( x )$ （20 $q ( x ) = r _ { 1 } ( x ) s _ { 2 } ( x ) + r _ { 2 } ( x ) ;$ （20   
》 Néu $r _ { 2 } ( x ) \neq 0$ thi $0 \neq \mathsf { b a c } r _ { 2 } ( x ) < \mathsf { b a c } r _ { 1 } ( x )$ ：   
$\blacktriangleright$ Tiép tuc.. $r _ { k - 2 } ( x ) = r _ { k - 1 } ( x ) s _ { k } ( x ) + r _ { k } ( x ) ,$ （20 $r _ { k - 1 } ( x ) = r _ { k } ( x ) s _ { k + 1 } ( x ) + c .$ （204号 ■ $U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) = U C L N ( q ( x ) , r _ { 1 } ( x ) ) = . . . = U C L N ( r _ { k } ( x ) , c ) .$ （204 ■ Néu c=0 thi rx(x)=UCLN(p(x),q(x)); $\cdot \ \mathrm { N } \acute { \hat { \mathrm { e u } } } \ c \neq 0 \ \mathrm { t h i } ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) = 1 .$

# Dinh ly 7:

1) $d ( x ) = U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) )$ khi va chi khi ton tai cac da thirc $u ( x ) , \nu ( x )$ sao cho $p ( x ) u ( x ) + q ( x ) \nu ( x ) = d ( x )$

2） $( p ( x ) , q ( x ) ) = 1$ khi va chi khi tön tai cac da thic $u ( x ) , \nu ( x )$ sao cho （24 $p ( x ) u ( x ) + q ( x ) \nu ( x ) = 1 .$

# Dinh ly 8:

1) Hai da thirc $p ( x ) , q ( x ) \in \mathbb { C } [ x ]$ cua truong só phic la nguyén tó cung nhau khi va chi khi khong co nghiém chung nao.

2) Hai da thirc $p ( x ) , q ( x ) \in \mathbb { R } \big [ x \big ]$ trén truong só thuc nguyen tó cung nhau khi va chi khi chung khong co nghiém thuc hay nghiém phirc chung nao.

He qua 9: $p ( x ) , q ( x )$ la hai da thirc trén truong só thuc hay só phirc thi:

${ \big ( } p ( x ) , q ( x ) { \big ) } = 1 \Longleftrightarrow { \big ( } p ^ { m } ( x ) , q ^ { n } ( x ) { \big ) } = 1$ , voi moi so nguyen duong m,n.

Nhan xét 7.1: Cac da thurc bat kha qui voi he só ung voi bac cao nhat bang 1 (chang han: $x - c ; x ^ { 2 } + p x + q , p ^ { 2 } - 4 q < 0$ ) dong vai tro nhu cac só nguyeén tó trong vanh $\mathbb { Z }$ . Vi vay ta có thé chuyén mót cach tuong tu cac két qua trong vanh só nguyén $\mathbb { Z }$ sang vanh nguyén các da thuc $K [ x ]$ . Chang han dé tim $d ( x ) = U C L N ( p ( x ) , q ( x ) )$ ta phan tich $p ( x )$ thanh tich cac da thurc bät kha qui. Khi d6 $d ( x )$ bang tich cac da thirc bat kha qui có mät dong thoi trong $p ( x )$ va $q ( x )$ ：

Vidu 7.9: $p ( x ) = \left( x - 2 \right) ^ { 3 } \left( x + 1 \right) ^ { 2 } \left( x ^ { 2 } + 2 x + 3 \right) ^ { 3 } ,$ （204号

$$
q ( x ) = { \left( x - 3 \right) } ^ { 2 } { \left( x + 1 \right) } ^ { 5 } { \left( x ^ { 2 } + 2 x + 3 \right) } \Rightarrow d ( x ) = { \left( x + 1 \right) } ^ { 2 } { \left( x ^ { 2 } + 2 x + 3 \right) } .
$$
